To our shareholders,                                                           致各股東：

In 2024, the global economic environment was complex and constantly            2024年，全球經濟環境複雜多變，中國經濟
evolving. The national economy showed great resilience despite external        在外部壓力與內部挑戰中展現出強大韌性，全
pressure and internal challenges, with a 5.0% gross domestic product           年GDP實現同比增長5.0%。受下游地產投資
(“GDP”) growth rate for the year. Affected by the decline in downstream        縮減和基建工程項目放緩影響，水泥市場需求
property investment and slowdown in infrastructure projects, the cement        延續前兩年走勢繼續下滑，下半年同業開始探
market demand continued the downward trend seen in the past two                索「反內卷」之道，叠加煤炭等主要原材料價格
years. In the second half of the year, industry players started to explore     同比下降，行業效益回升，降幅收窄。
ways to counter “involution”. Coupled with the year-on-year decrease
in the prices of major raw materials such as coal, the cement industry’s
profits rebounded and the rate of decline was narrowed.

At present, low-carbon development and reduction of excess production          當下，低碳發展與化解過剩產能成為水泥行業
capacity have become the key driving force behind supply-side changes in       供給變化的關鍵驅動力。國務院發佈的《2024-
the cement industry. The 2024-2025 Action Plan for Energy Conservation         2025年節能降碳行動方案》明確提出，到2025
and Carbon Reduction issued by the State Council of the People’s Republic      年底，全國水泥熟料產能將控制在18億噸左
of China clearly stated the national cement and clinker production capacity    右。與此同時，生態環境部推動水泥行業超低
will be controlled at approximately 1.8 billion tonnes by the end of 2025.     排放改造，力爭到2025年底前完成50%熟料
At the same time, the Ministry of Ecology and Environment promoted             產能的改造任務。這些政策的實施，不僅加速
ultra-low emission transformation of the cement industry and strived to        行業綠色轉型，也為具備技術優勢的企業提供
complete the upgrade of 50% of clinker production capacity by the end          新的發展機遇。
of 2025. The implementation of these policies will not only accelerated
green transformation of the industry, but also provide new development
opportunities for enterprises with technological advantages.

Group’s revenue growth. The horizontal cooperation with the affiliated         推行「統購+智慧」的採購戰略，建立中央庫

           As price war was eased, cement price continued to rise,              計2025年，隨著企業持續響應

企業管治報告

The Board is committed to maintaining high standards of corporate               董事會致力維持高水平之企業管治常規，以保
governance practices to safeguard the interests of the Company’s                障本公司股東權益，並提升企業價值及問責
shareholders and to enhance corporate value and accountability. These           性。有關目標可透過高效率之董事會、問責清
can be achieved by an effective Board, segregation of duties with clear         晰且權責分明之職務、良好內部監控、適當風
accountability, sound internal control, appropriate risk assessment             險評估程序及對全體股東之透明度而達致。
procedures and transparency to all the shareholders.

Corporate governance is the process by which the Board instructs                企業管治是董事會指導集團管理層處理事務以
management of the Group to conduct its affairs with a view to ensuring          確保實現目標的過程。董事會致力於維護及發
that its objectives are met. The Board is committed to maintaining and          展穩健的企業管治實踐，以確保：
developing robust corporate governance practices that are intended to
ensure:

–      satisfactory and sustainable returns to shareholders;                    －     為股東提供滿意及可持續的回報；

–      that the interests of those who deal with the Company are                －     與公司有業務來往者的利益得到保障；
       safeguarded;

–      that overall business risk is understood and managed appropriately;      －     了解並適當地管理整體業務風險；
       and

–      the delivery of high-quality products and services to the satisfaction   －     提供顧客滿意的優質產品與服務；及
       of customers; and

–      that high standards of ethics are maintained.                            －     維持高商業道德標準。

The Company has established a Board Independence Evaluation                 本公司已建立董事會獨立性評審機制，制定流
Mechanism which sets out the processes and procedures to ensure             程及程序以確保董事會具有穩固的獨立性，讓
a strong independent element on the Board, which allows the Board           董事會能夠有效地作出獨立判斷，以更有效的
effectively exercises independent judgment to better safeguard              保障股東利益。
Shareholders’ interests.

The objectives of the evaluation are to improve Board effectiveness,        評審的目標旨在改善董事會的效率、使能更好
maximise strengths, and identify the areas that need improvement or         體現其優勢及識別需改善或進一步發展的領
further development. The evaluation process also clarifies what actions     域。評審過程釐清本公司於維持及改善董事會
of the Company need to be taken to maintain and improve the Board           績效，例如，解決各董事的個人培訓和發展需
performance, for instance, addressing individual training and development   求。
needs of each Director.

Pursuant to the Board Independence Evaluation Mechanism, the Board          根據董事會獨立性評審機制，董事會將就其獨
will conduct annual review on its independence. The Board Independence      立性進行年度檢討。董事會獨立性評審報告將
Evaluation Report will be presented to the Board which will collectively    提交至董事會，董事會將集體討論結果和改進
discuss the results and the action plan for improvement, if appropriate.    行動計劃，如果適用。

and supplemented by individual interviews. The Board Independence           董事會獨立性評審報告已提交至董事會，評審

reference to the independence guidelines set out in the Listing Rules,          引，候選人可在資歷、技能、經驗、獨立性及

willingness and ability to devote adequate time to discharge duties as a        並履行相關職責。
member of the Board.

preference based on the needs of the Company and reference check of             其進行篩選。然後，提名委員會應建議董事會
each candidate. The Nomination Committee should then recommend                  指定適當的董事候選人。對於任何由股東提名
to the Board to appoint the appropriate candidate for directorship, as          在公司股東大會上選舉為董事的人，提名委員
applicable. For any person that is nominated by a shareholder for election      會應根據上述標準對該候選人進行評估，以確

time certain regulatory measures as appropriate to prevent violation of the    監管措施，以防止違反本集團的披露規定，包
disclosure requirements of the Group, including:                               括：

       and Directors) have access to inside information upon request.               事）可應要求獲取內幕消息。接觸內幕
       Employees having access to the inside information are fully aware            消息的員工均充份了解其保密責任。
       of their confidentiality responsibilities.

–      All employees (including the Directors of the Group) must               －    所有員工（包括本集團董事）須嚴格遵守
       strictly comply with the employment provisions regarding the                 相關的保密信息管理僱傭細則。
       administration of confidential information.

The Group complies with the requirements of the Securities and Futures         本集團遵守證券及期貨條例及上市規則的規
Ordinance (SFO) and the Listing Rules. The Group discloses inside              定。除非該消息屬於證券及期貨條例規定的
information to the public as soon as reasonably practicable unless             豁免範圍內，本集團在合理可行的情況下會盡
the information falls within the safe harbours as provided in the SFO.         快向公眾披露內幕消息。在向公眾全面披露信
Before the information is fully disclosed to the public, the Group ensures     息前，本集團確保信息嚴格保密。如本集團認
the information is kept strictly confidential. If the Group believes that      為無法保持必要的保密程度或可能違反保密規
the necessary degree of confidentiality cannot be maintained or that           定，本集團將立即向公眾披露信息。本集團致
confidentiality may have been breached, the Group would immediately            力確保公告或通函所載資料不會就重大事實包

that information contained in announcements or circulars is not false or       虛假或誤導陳述，並以清晰及平衡的方式呈
misleading as to material facts, or false or misleading through the omission   列，均等披露正負面事實。
of material facts, and presenting information in a clear and balanced way,
which requires equal disclosure of both positive and negative facts.

OPINION                                                                      意見
We have audited the consolidated financial statements of Asia Cement         我們已審計載於第67至187頁亞洲水泥（中國）
(China) Holdings Corporation (the “Company”) and its subsidiaries            控股公司（「貴公司」）及其附屬公司（以下統稱
(collectively referred to as the “Group”) set out on pages 67 to 187,        「貴集團」）的綜合財務報表，包括於2024年12
which comprise the consolidated statement of financial position as at        月31日的綜合財務狀況表、截至該日止年度的
31 December 2024, and the consolidated statement of profit or loss           綜合損益及其他全面收益表、綜合權益變動表
and other comprehensive income, consolidated statement of changes in         及綜合現金流量表以及綜合財務報表附註（包
equity and consolidated statement of cash flows for the year then ended,     括主要會計政策概要）。
and notes to the consolidated financial statements, including material
accounting policy information and other explanatory information.

In our opinion, the consolidated financial statements give a true and        我們認為，綜合財務報表已根據國際會計準則
fair view of the consolidated financial position of the Group as at 31       委員會（「國際會計準則委員會」）頒佈的國際財
December 2024, and of its consolidated financial performance and its         務報告準則（「國際財務報告準則」）真實而公允
consolidated cash flows for the year then ended in accordance with           地反映 貴集團於2024年12月31日的綜合財
International Financial Reporting Standards (“IFRSs”) issued by the          務狀況及其截至該日止年度的綜合財務表現及
International Accounting Standards Board (the “IASB”) and have been          綜合現金流量，並已遵守香港公司條例的披露
properly prepared in compliance with the disclosure requirements of the      規定妥為編製。

 Estimated provision of expected credit losses (“ECL”) for trade receivables
 交易應收款項的預期信貸損失評估

 With the continued impact of the property market recession      由於2024年房地產市場衰退及經濟復甦乏力影響，分
 and the sluggish recovery on the economy in 2024, the           別作為本集團間接客戶和最終客戶的房地產開發商和建
 property developers and the constructors who were the           造商面臨龐大債務及現金流問題。截至2024年12月31
 indirect and ultimate customers of the Group respectively       日，本集團應收款項扣除人民幣203,671,000元準備後
 faced massive debt and cash flow issues. As at 31 December      的淨額約為人民幣298,334,000元。於截至2024年12月
 2024, the trade receivables of the Group was approximately      31日止年度，本集團撥回人民幣16,803,000元的應收款
 RMB298,334,000, net of allowance amounting to                   項預期信貸虧損。
 RMB203,671,000. During the year ended 31 December
 2024, the Group reversed RMB16,803,000 of ECL of trade
 receivables.

 receivables to the Group’s consolidated financial position      整體極其重要，加上管理層在使用預期信貸虧損評估本
 and the involvement of subjective judgement and                 集團交易應收款項的減值虧損時，涉及管理層的主觀判

 statements, management of the Group estimates the               賬齡及還款記錄後，通過對具有共同風險特徵的債務人
 amount of lifetime ECL of trade receivables other than          進行分組，再根據撥備矩陣模型估算交易應收款項的全

 history and past due status of respective trade receivables.    評估。信貸減值的交易應收款項的撥備金額乃按資產賬
 Estimated loss rates are based on historical observed default   面值與估計未來現金流量現值之間的差額，並考慮預期
 rates over the expected life of the debtors and are adjusted    未來信貸虧損來計算。
 for forward-looking information. Trade receivables that are
 credit-impaired are separately assessed for ECL individually.
 The loss allowance amount of the credit-impaired trade
 receivables is measured as the difference between the asset’s
 gross amount and the present value of estimated future
 cash flows with the consideration of expected future credit
 losses.

            Basis of consolidation                                                      綜合基準
            The consolidated financial statements incorporate the                       綜合財務報表包括本公司及由
            financial statements of the Company and entities controlled                 本公司及其附屬公司所控制實
            by the Company and its subsidiaries. Control is achieved                    體之財務報表。倘本公司達成
            where the Company:                                                          以下事項，則視為取得控制權：

            •      has power over the investee;                                         •   對被投資方擁有權力；

            •      is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its              •   就參與被投資方經營所
                   involvement with the investee; and                                       得可變回報面臨風險或
                                                                                            擁有權利；及

            •      has the ability to use its power to affect its returns.              •   擁有行使其權力以影響
                                                                                            其回報之能力。

            The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an investee                 倘有事實及情況顯示上文所列
            if facts and circumstances indicate that there are changes to               三項控制元素之一項或多項有
            one or more of the three elements of control listed above.                  變，則本集團會重估其是否控
                                                                                        制被投資方。

            Consolidation of a subsidiary begins when the Group                         本集團取得附屬公司控制權時
            obtains control over the subsidiaries and ceases when the                   開始將附屬公司綜合入賬，於
            Group loses control of the subsidiary. Specifically, income                 喪失附屬公司控制權時終止入
            and expenses of a subsidiary acquired or disposed of during                 賬。具體而言，年內收購或出
            the years are included in the consolidated statement of                     售之附屬公司收入及開支自本
            profit or loss and other comprehensive income from the                      集團取得控制權當日及直至本
            date the Group gains control until the date when the                        集團不再控制附屬公司之日止
            Group ceases to control the subsidiary.                                     計入綜合損益及其他全面收益
                                                                                        表。

            Profit or loss and each item of other comprehensive income                  損益及其他全面收入各項目歸
            are attributed to the owners of the Company and to the                      屬於本公司擁有人及非控股權
            non-controlling interests. Total comprehensive income of                    益。附屬公司之全面收入總額
            subsidiaries is attributed to the owners of the Company and                 歸屬於本公司擁有人及非控股
            to the non-controlling interests even if this results in the                權益，即使此會導致非控股權
            non-controlling interests having a deficit balance.                         益出現虧絀結餘。

            When necessary, adjustments are made to the financial                       如有需要，附屬公司財務報表
            statements of subsidiaries to bring their accounting policies               會作出調整，以使其會計政策
            in line with the Group’s accounting policies.                               與本集團其他成員公司所用者
                                                                                        一致。

            All intragroup assets and liabilities, equity, income                       所有集團內公司間之交易結
            and expenses and cash flows relating to transactions                        餘、收入及開支以及與本集團

            consolidation.                                                              量均於綜合時悉數對銷。

            Goodwill                                                                     商譽
            Goodwill arising on an acquisition of a business is carried at               收購業務所產生之商譽按收購
            cost as established at the date of acquisition of the business               業務當日所確立之成本減累計

            (“CGU”) (or Group’s of cash-generating units (“CGUs”))                       之本集團各現金產生單位（或各
            that is expected to benefit from the synergies of the                        組現金產生單位），即就內部管
            combination, which represent the lowest level at which the                   理而言為商譽之最低水平且不
            goodwill is monitored for internal management purpose                        大於經營分部。
            and not larger than an operating segment.

            frequently when there is indication that the unit may                       進行減值測試，或於有跡象顯
            be impaired. For goodwill arising on an acquisition in a                    示單位可能出現減值時更頻繁
            reporting period, the CGU (or group of CGUs) to which                       地進行減值測試。就於報告期
            goodwill has been allocated is tested for impairment                        間進行收購產生之商譽而言，
            before the end of that reporting period. If the recoverable                 獲分配商譽之現金產生單位（或
            amount is less than its carrying amount, the impairment                     各組現金產生單位）於該報告期
            loss is allocated first to reduce the carrying amount of any                間結算日前進行減值測試。倘
            goodwill and then to the other assets on a pro-rata basis                   現金產生單位（或各組現金產生
            based on the carrying amount of each asset in the unit (or                  單 位 ）之 可 收 回 金 額 低 於 賬 面
            group of CGUs).                                                             值，則會先行分配減值虧損，
                                                                                        以削減該單位獲分配之任何商

            Investments in associates and joint ventures                                於聯營公司及合營企業之投資
            An associate is an entity over which the Group has                          聯營公司為本集團對其有重大
            significant influence. Significant influence is the power to                影響力之實體。重大影響力為
            participate in the financial and operating policy decisions                 有權參與被投資方之財務及營
            of the investee but is not control or joint control over those              運政策決定，惟並非控制或共
            policies.                                                                   同控制該等政策。

            A joint venture is a joint arrangement whereby the parties                  合營企業為對安排擁有共同控
            that have joint control of the arrangement have rights to                   制權之各方對共同安排之資產
            the net assets of the joint arrangement. Joint control is the               淨值擁有權利之共同安排。共
            contractually agreed sharing of control of an arrangement,                  同控制權為合約約定對安排所
            which exists only when decisions about the relevant                         共有之控制權，其僅在相關業
            activities require unanimous consent of the parties sharing                 務決策須取得共有控制權各方
            control.                                                                    之一致同意時存在。

            The results and assets and liabilities of associates and joint              聯營公司及合營企業之業績及
            ventures are incorporated in the consolidated financial                     資產與負債採用權益會計法計
            statements using the equity method of accounting. The                       入該等綜合財務報表。用作權
            financial statements of associates and joint ventures                       益會計用途之聯營公司及合營
            used for equity accounting purposes are prepared using                      企業財務報表使用本集團在類
            uniform accounting policies as those of the Group for like                  似情況下就同類交易及事項所
            transactions and events in similar circumstances. Under                     用之統一會計政策編製。根據
            the equity method, an investment in an associate or a joint                 權益法，於聯營公司或合營企
            venture is initially recognised in the consolidated statement               業之投資初步按成本於綜合財
            of financial position at cost and adjusted thereafter to                    務狀況報表中確認並其後作出
            recognise the Group’s share of the profit or loss and other                 調整，以確認本集團應佔聯營
            comprehensive income of the associate or joint venture.                     公司或合營企業之損益及其他
            Changes in net assets of the associate/joint venture other                  全面收入。倘本集團應佔聯營
            than profit or loss and other comprehensive income are not                  公司或合營企業之虧損超出其
            accounted for unless such changes resulted in changes in                    於該聯營公司或合營企業之權
            ownership interest held by the Group. When the Group’s                      益（包括實際組成本集團於該聯
            share of losses of an associate or joint venture exceeds the                營公司或合營企業之投資淨值
            Group’s interest in that associate or joint venture (which                  其中部分之任何長期權益），則
            includes any long-term interests that, in substance, form                   本集團終止確認其應佔之進一
            part of the Group’s net investment in the associate or joint                步虧損。僅當本集團產生法定
            venture), the Group discontinues recognising its share of                   或推定責任或代表該聯營公司
            further losses. Additional losses are recognised only to the                或合營企業付款時，方會確認
            extent that the Group has incurred legal or constructive                    額外虧損。
            obligations or made payments on behalf of the associate or
            joint venture.

            For a contract that contains a lease component and one or                  倘合約包含一個租賃成分及一
            more additional lease or non-lease components, the Group                   個或多個額外的租賃或非租賃
            allocates the consideration in the contract to each lease                  成分，則本集團應基於租賃成
            component on the basis of the relative stand-alone price of                分之相關單獨價格及非租賃成
            the lease component and the aggregate stand-alone price                    分之單獨價格總和，將合約對
            of the non-lease components.                                               價在各租賃成分之間進行分攤。

            The Group applies the practical expedient not to separate                  本集團亦應用可行權宜方法，
            non-lease components from lease component, and instead                     不從租賃成分中區分非租賃成
            account for the lease component and any associated non-                    分，而是將租賃成分及任何相
            lease components as a single lease component.                              關非租賃成分入賬列作單一租
                                                                                       賃成分。

            Short-term leases and leases of low-value assets                           短期租賃及低價值資產租賃
            The Group applies the short-term lease recognition                         對於租期自開始日期起計為
            exemption to leases of office and dormitories that have a                  十二個月或以內且不包含購買
            lease term of 12 months or less from the commencement                      選擇權之辦公室物業之租賃，
            date and do not contain a purchase option. It also applies                 本集團應用短期租賃確認豁
            the recognition exemption for lease of low-value assets.                   免。其對低價值資產之租賃亦
            Lease payments on short-term leases and leases of low-                     應用確認豁免。短期租賃及低
            value assets are recognised as expense on a straight-line                  價值資產租賃之租賃付款按直
            basis or another systematic basis over the lease term.                     線法或另一有系統基準於租期
                                                                                       內確認為開支。

            Taxation                                                                       稅項
            Income tax expense represents the sum of current and                           所得稅開支指即期應付稅項與
            deferred income tax expense.                                                   遞延稅項之總和。

            The tax currently payable is based on taxable profit for                       即期應付稅項按年內應課稅溢
            the year. Taxable profit differs from profit/(loss) before                     利 計 算 。 由 於 應 課 稅 溢 利（ 虧
            tax because of income or expense that are taxable or                           損 ）不 包 括 其 他 年 度 應 課 稅 或
            deductible in other years and items that are never taxable or                  可扣稅之收入或開支項目，亦
            deductible. The Group’s liability for current tax is calculated                不包括毋須課稅或不可扣稅項
            using tax rates that have been enacted or substantively                        目，故應課稅溢利與於綜合損
            enacted by the end of the reporting period.                                    益及其他全面收益表呈報之「除
                                                                                           稅前溢利」不同。本集團之即期
                                                                                           稅項負債按於報告期間結算日
                                                                                           已頒佈或實質頒佈之稅率計算。

            Deferred tax is recognised on temporary differences                            遞延稅項按綜合財務報表內資
            between the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities in the                  產及負債賬面值與計算應課稅
            consolidated financial statements and the corresponding tax                    溢利所用相應稅基間之暫時差
            bases used in the computation of taxable profit. Deferred                      額確認。所有應課稅暫時差額
            tax liabilities are generally recognised for all taxable                       一般確認為遞延稅項負債。倘
            temporary differences. Deferred tax assets are generally                       應課稅溢利可能抵銷可扣減暫
            recognised for all deductible temporary differences to the                     時差額，則所有可扣減暫時差
            extent that it is probable that taxable profits will be available              額一般確認為遞延稅項資產。
            against which those deductible temporary differences can                       倘其他資產與負債之商譽或初
            be utilised. Such deferred tax assets and liabilities are not                  步確認（而非於業務合併中）於
            recognised if the temporary difference arises from goodwill                    交易中所產生之暫時差額不影
            or from the initial recognition (other than in a business                      響應課稅溢利或會計溢利，則
            combination) of other assets and liabilities in a transaction                  不會確認有關資產與負債並且
            that affects neither the taxable profit nor the accounting                     在交易時不會產生相等的應課
            profit and at the time of the transaction does not give rise                   稅和可抵減暫時性差異。此外，
            to equal taxable and deductible temporary differences. In                      如果暫時差額產生於商譽的初
            addition, deferred tax liabilities are not recognised if the                   步確認，則不確認遞延稅項負
            temporary difference arises from the initial recognition of                    債。
            goodwill.

            difference and it is probable that the temporary difference                    時差額之撥回，且有關暫時差

            arising from deductible temporary differences associated                       除外。與該等投資及權益相關

            the extent that it is probable that there will be sufficient                   稅項資產，僅於可能有足夠應

            Taxation (continued)                                                           稅項（續）
            The carrying amount of deferred tax assets is reviewed at                      本集團於各報告期間結算日會
            the end of the reporting period and reduced to the extent                      檢討遞延稅項資產之賬面值，
            that it is no longer probable that sufficient taxable profits                  並於不再可能有足夠應課稅溢
            will be available to allow all or part of the asset to be                      利收回全部或部分資產時扣減。
            recovered.

            Deferred tax assets and liabilities are measured at the tax                    遞延稅項資產及負債按預期於
            rates that are expected to apply in the period in which the                    清償負債或變現資產期間適用
            liability is settled or the asset is realised, based on tax rates              之稅率，以於報告期間結算日
            (and tax laws) that have been enacted or substantively                         已頒佈或實質頒佈之稅率（及稅
            enacted by the end of the reporting period.                                    法）為基準計量。

            The measurement of deferred tax liabilities and assets                         遞延稅項負債及資產之計量反
            reflects the tax consequences that would follow from the                       映於各報告期間結算日將自本
            manner in which the Group expects, at the end of each                          集團預期收回或清償其資產與
            reporting period, to recover or settle the carrying amount                     負債賬面值方式產生之稅務後
            of its assets and liabilities.                                                 果。

            For the purposes of measuring deferred tax for investment                      為計量使用公允價值模型計量
            properties that are measured using the fair value model,                       的投資性房地產的遞延所得
            the carrying amounts of such properties are presumed to                        稅，除非該推定被推翻，否則
            be recovered entirely through sale, unless the presumption is                  該等房地產的賬面價值應推定
            rebutted. The presumption is rebutted when the investment                      為完全通過出售收回。當投資
            property is depreciable and is held within a business model                    性房地產可折舊，且其目標是
            whose objective is to consume substantially all of the                         隨著時間的推移而實質上消耗
            economic benefits embodied in the investment property                          投資性房地產所體現的所有經
            over time, rather than through sale.                                           濟利益，而不是通過出售，則
                                                                                           該假設被推翻。

            For the purposes of measuring deferred tax for leasing                         就計量本集團確認使用權資產
            transactions in which the Group recognises the right-of-                       及相關租賃負債之租賃交易之
            use assets and the related lease liabilities, the Group first                  遞延稅項而言，本集團首先確
            determines whether the tax deductions are attributable to                      定減稅額應歸屬於使用權資產
            the right-of-use assets or the lease liabilities.                              或租賃負債。

            For leasing transactions in which the tax deductions are                       就租賃負債應佔稅項減項之租
            attributable to the lease liabilities, the Group applies IAS                   賃交易而言，本集團分別應用
            12 requirements to the lease liabilities and the related                       國際會計準則第12號所得稅有
            assets separately. The Group recognises a deferred tax                         關所有權資產及租賃負債之規
            asset related to lease liabilities to the extent that it is                    定。本集團確認有關租賃負債
            probable that taxable profit will be available against which                   的一項遞延稅項資產（以可能獲
            the deductible temporary difference can be utilised and a                      得可抵扣臨時稅差異的應課稅

            The effective interest method is a method of calculating                      實際利率法為計算金融資產或
            the amortised cost of a financial asset or financial liability                金融負債攤銷成本及於有關期
            and of allocating interest income and interest expense over                   間分配利息收入及利息開支之
            the relevant period. The effective interest rate is the rate                  方法。實際利率為於金融資產
            that exactly discounts estimated future cash receipts and                     或 金 融 負 債 預 計 年 期 或（ 如 適
            payments (including all fees and points paid or received                      用）較短期間內準確貼現估計未
            that form an integral part of the effective interest rate,                    來現金付款（包括構成實際利率
            transaction costs and other premiums or discounts) through                    其中一部分之所有已付或已收
            the expected life of the financial asset or financial liability,              費用及點子、交易成本及其他
            or, where appropriate, a shorter period, to the net carrying                  溢價或折讓）至初步確認時之賬
            amount on initial recognition.                                                面淨值之利率。

            Financial assets                                                              金融資產
            Classification and subsequent measurement of financial                        金融資產之分類及其後計量
            assets

            subsequently measured at amortised cost:                                      按攤銷成本計量：

            •      the financial asset is held within a business model                     •     目的為收取合約現金流
                   whose objective is to collect contractual cash flows;                         量之業務模式中持有之
                   and                                                                           金融資產；及

            •      the contractual terms give rise on specified dates to                   •     合約條款於指定日期產
                   cash flows that are solely payments of principal and                          生之現金流量僅為支付
                   interest on the principal amount outstanding.                                 本金及未償還本金之利
                                                                                                 息。

            Financial assets that meet the following conditions are                        符合以下條件之金融資產其後
            subsequently measured at fair value through other                              按公平值計入其他全面收益
            comprehensive income (“FVTOCI”):                                              （「 按 公 平 值 計 入 其 他 全 面 收
                                                                                           益」）計量：

            •      the financial asset is held within a business model                     •     通過出售及收取合同現
                   whose objective is achieved by both collecting                                金流量達成目的之業務
                   contractual cash flows and selling the financial                              模式中持有之金融資
                   assets; and                                                                   產；及

            •      the contractual terms give rise on specified dates to                   •     合同條款於指定日期產
                   cash flows that are solely payments of principal and                          生之現金流量僅為支付
                   interest on the principal amount outstanding.                                 尚未償還本金額之本金
                                                                                                 及利息。

                                                                                                        難；

                                                                                                        況；

                    (c)    the lender(s) of the borrower, for economic                           (c)    向借款人作出貸
                           or contractual reasons relating to the                                       款之貸款人出於
                           borrower’s financial difficulty, having                                      與借款人財政困
                           granted to the borrower a concession(s) that                                 難有關之經濟或

                   counterparty has been placed under liquidation                             進 入 破 產 程 序 ）， 本 集
                   or has entered into bankruptcy proceedings.                                團會撇銷金融資產。經
                   Financial assets written off may still be subject to                       計及在適當情況下之法

            (v)    Measurement and recognition of ECL                                    (v)   計量及確認預期信貸虧

                   The measurement of ECL is a function of the                                 預期信貸虧損之計量乃

                   magnitude of the loss if there is a default) and                            率（ 即 違 約 造 成 虧 損 的
                   the exposure at default. The assessment of the                              幅 度 ）及 違 約 風 險 之 函
                   probability of default and loss given default is                            數。違約概率及違約虧
                   based on historical data and forward-looking                                損率乃根據經前瞻性資
                   information. Estimation of ECL reflects an unbiased                         料調整之歷史數據進行
                   and probability-weighted amount that is determined                          評估。預期信貸虧損之
                   with the respective risks of default occurring as the                       估計反映無偏頗及概率
                   weights. Trade receivables that are credit-impaired                         加權數額，其乃根據加
                   are assessed for ECL individually. In addition, the                         權之相應違約風險而釐
                   Group uses a practical expedient in estimating                              定。本集團經考慮過往
                   ECL on trade receivables which are not assessed                             信貸虧損經驗後使用撥
                   individually using a provision matrix taking into                           備矩陣並採用可行權宜
                   consideration historical credit loss experience,                            之計估計交易應收款項
                   adjusted for forward looking information that is                            之預期信貸虧損，並按
                   available without undue cost or effort.                                     毋需花費不必要成本或

                                                                                               料調整。

      other sources. The estimates and associated assumptions are based            往經驗及被視為相關之其他因素為基

      going basis. Revisions to accounting estimates are recognised in             計估計的修訂僅影響目前期間，則會該
      the period in which the estimate is revised if the revision affects          期間確認會計估計的修訂；倘有關修訂
      only that period, or in the period of the revision and future periods        影響目前及未來期間，則在修訂及未來
      if the revision affects both current and future periods.                     期間確認會計估計的修訂。

      Internal reports that are regularly reviewed by the chief executive         行政總裁（即主要營運決策者）（「主要營
      officer, being the chief operating decision maker (the “CODM”),             運決策者」）就分配資源及評估分部表現

      segment performance focus on the revenue and operating results              業務及混凝土業務之收益及經營業績。
      of the cement business and the concrete business, respectively.             根據國際財務報告準則第8號，其被視
      They are considered as the reportable segments of the Group                 為本集團經營分部及呈報分部。有關各
      under IFRS 8. Information relating to assets and liabilities in each        分部資產及負債之資料並不計入主要營

      by the CODM.

      The cement business segment and the concrete business segment               水泥業務分部及混凝土業務分部均包括
      both include a number of operations in various cities within PRC            於中國境內不同城市的多個業務營運，
      each of which is considered as a separate operating segment of              各自被視為主要營運決策者的獨立營運
      the CODM. For financial statements presentation purpose, these              分部。為呈列財務報表之目的，該等獨
      individual operating segments have been aggregated into the                 立營運分部經考慮下列因素後合計為水
      cement business segment or the concrete business segment by                 泥業務分部或混凝土業務分部：
      taking into account the following factors:

      Typical payment term which impact on the amount of contract                     對已確認的合同負債金額產生影響的典
      liabilities recognised is as follows:                                           型付款方式如下：

      The Group enters into goods supply contracts with customers and                 本集團與客戶訂立貨物供貨合同，並收
      receives advance payments from customers which are interest-free.               取客戶免息之預付款項。當本集團在交
      When the Group receives a deposit before the delivery of goods,                 付貨物之前收到定金時，這將在合同開
      this will give rise to contract liabilities at the start of a contract,         始時產生合同負債，直到相關合同確認

40. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                                   40. 資本風險管理
      The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in the Group           本集團管理其資本以確保本集團內之實
      will be able to continue as a going concern while maximising the             體能夠持續經營，並透過優化債務及權
      return to shareholders through the optimisation of the debt and              益結餘盡量增加股東回報。本集團整體
      equity balance. The Group’s overall strategy remains unchanged               策略於有關期間維持不變。
      throughout the relevant periods.

      The capital structure of the Group consists of net debt that includes        本集團之資本架構包含債務淨額（包括
      the borrowings and lease liabilities disclosed in note 30 and note           分別於附註30及31披露之借貸及租賃
      31 respectively, net of bank balances and equity attributable to             負債），扣除銀行結餘及本公司擁有人
      owners of the Company, comprising issued share capital, reserves             應佔權益（包括已發行股本、儲備及保
      and retained earnings.                                                       留溢利）。

      The directors of the Company review the capital structure from               董事不時檢討資本架構。作為檢討之一
      time to time. As a part of this review, the directors of the Company         部分，董事考慮資本成本及各類資本相
      consider the cost of capital and the risks associated with each              關之風險。本集團將根據董事建議，透
      class of capital. Based on recommendations of the directors of               過派付股息、發行新股、新造債務或贖
      the Company, the Group will balance its overall capital structure            回現有債務，平衡其整體資本架構。
      through the payment of dividends, the issue of new shares, new
      debts or the redemption of existing debts.

            In order to minimise the credit risk, management of the                     為盡量減低信貸風險，本集團
            Group has delegated a team responsible for determination                    管理層已委任一組人員負責釐
            of credit limits and credit approvals. Before accepting                     定信貸限額及批核信貸。於接
            any new customer, the Group uses an internal credit                         受任何新客戶前，本集團採用
            scoring system to assess the potential customer’s credit                    內部信貸評級系統評估潛在客
            quality and defines credit limits by customer. Limits and                   戶之信貸質素及界定客戶之信
            scoring attributed to customers are reviewed once a                         貸額度，並每年一次審閱客戶
            year. Other monitoring procedures are in place to ensure                    信貸額度及評級。另外制訂其
            that follow-up action is taken to recover overdue debts.                    他監控程序，以確保採取跟進
            In this regard, the directors of the Company consider                       行動，追收逾期債務。就此而
            that the Group’s credit risk is significantly reduced. In                   言，本公司董事認為信貸風險
            addition, the Group has applied the simplified approach                     已大幅減少。此外，本集團已
            in IFRS 9 to measure the credit loss allowance at lifetime                  採納國際財務報告準則第9號之
            ECL. Except for trade receivables that are credit-impaired                  簡化方法來計量全期預期信貸
            are assessed individually, the Group determines the ECL                     虧損。除具有重大未償還及特
            on the remaining balances by using a provision matrix                       殊信貸風險結餘（單獨評估）之
            grouped by common risk characteristic. As part of the                       交易應收款項外，本集團採用
            Group’s credit risk management, the Group uses debtors’                     按共同風險特徵分組的撥備矩
            aging, repayment history and past due status to assess the                  陣確定餘額的預期信貸虧損。
            impairment for its customers in relation to its operation                   作為本集團信貸風險管理的一
            because these customers consist of a large number of                        部分，本集團使用債務人賬
            small customers with common risk characteristics that are                   齡、還款歷史及逾期情形來評
            representative of the customers’ abilities to pay all amounts               估客戶與其經營相關的減值，
            due in accordance with the contractual terms. The estimated                 因為這些客戶包括大量具有共
            loss rates are estimated based on historically observed                     同風險特徵的小客戶，其代表
            default rates over the expected life of the debtors and are                 客戶依合約條款支付所有到期
            adjusted for forward-looking information that is available                  款項的能力。估計虧損率根據
            without undue cost or effort, such as economic data and                     債務人預期年期內之歷史觀察
            forecasts published by governmental bodies and industrial                   違約率估計，並就毋須付出不
            information published by relevant institutions. At every                    必要成本或努力即可取得之前
            reporting date, the historically observed default rates are                 瞻性資料作出調整，例如政府
            reassessed and changes in the forward-looking information                   機構發佈之經濟數據和預測以
            are considered.                                                             及相關機構發佈之行業資料。
                                                                                        在每一報告日期，歷史觀察違
                                                                                        約率會作重新評估，並考慮前
                                                                                        瞻性資料之變動。
